### Live-verification list

**Update — a live backend with the full §U-d contract was actually running on `:8080` (via `ng
serve`'s proxy) partway through this task, uncommitted work from the concurrent backend agent; items
1–5 below were re-verified against it directly with `curl`, not left as untested assumptions. Item 6
still applies unchanged.**

1. **`POST /api/devices/probe`** — **verified live, and it caught a real bug, fixed this cycle**: a
   probe against `{"protocol":"sim","uri":"sim://demo"}` returned
   `{"ok":true,"widthPx":640,"heightPx":480,"codec":"mjpeg","telemetryDetected":true,
   "frameJpegBase64":"…"}` — **`warnings` entirely absent**, not `[]`, contradicting
   `dto.ProbeDeviceResponse`'s own Javadoc ("always present, possibly empty"). `models.ts#ProbeDeviceResult.warnings`
   is now typed optional and `onboarding.html` guards it (`result.warnings && result.warnings.length
   > 0`) — see that type's own doc comment for the exact observed payload. A probe against a
   deliberately-bad URI (`rtsp://256.256.256.256:1/nope`) returned `422` with a specific
   `ErrorResponse.message` ("Could not connect: avformat_open_input() error -5: …") — confirmed
   `describeHttpError`'s existing default branch surfaces it verbatim, no new case needed. `ok` was
   never observed `false` on a 200 in this session (every failure came back 422 instead, matching the
   Javadoc's "a failed probe never reaches this type, it throws instead") — `OnboardingStore#probe`'s
   defensive `!result.ok` branch is now believed dead code in practice, but left in as harmless
   belt-and-suspenders.
2. **`PUT /api/assets/{id}/image`** — **verified live, full round-trip**: `PUT` a real JPEG →
   `204`; `GET /api/assets/{id}` immediately showed `hasImage: true` on the very next fetch (not
   eventually-consistent); `GET /api/assets/{id}/image` → `200`, `content-type: image/jpeg`, byte-identical
   body; `DELETE` → `204`; a subsequent `GET` → `404`. Angular's `HttpClient.put(url, blob)` was not
   exercised end-to-end from the actual Angular app in this session (the round-trip above used `curl`
   directly against the same endpoints `VisionApi.uploadAssetImage`/`assetImageUrl` build) — still
   worth a from-the-browser confirmation once a full onboarding pass is run manually, along with
   `downscaleImageToJpeg`'s own quality-ladder behavior against a genuinely large real photo (jsdom
   has no canvas, so that half is untested by `npm run test:ci` regardless).
3. **`AssetSummary.hasImage`/`AssetDetails.hasImage`** — **verified live** as part of item 2's
   round-trip above; also present (`false`) on a pre-existing asset that had never had a photo,
   confirming the field is unconditionally emitted, not only once a photo exists.
4. **`POST /api/assets` with an embedded device** (both the Register/Discover path and the wizard's
   `synthetic` simulate mode's own reroute use this identical shape) — **verified live**:
   `{"displayName":"Test Falcon","category":"drone","attributes":{"registrationNumber":"N12345"},
   "devices":[{"name":"Test Falcon","protocol":"sim","uri":"sim://demo"}]}` → `201`, a full
   `AssetDetails` with the device already attached (`capabilities:["VIDEO"]`, `state:"ACTIVE"`) and
   `attributes.registrationNumber` round-tripped intact. Cleaned up afterward (`DELETE
   /api/assets/{id}`, confirmed `200`) so no test debris was left in the shared dev backend.
5. **`PATCH /api/assets/{id}` attributes as a full replacement, not a merge** — **verified live,
   confirming the exact risk this section's own asset-detail write-up flags**: with
   `attributes: {registrationNumber: "N12345"}` already set, `PATCH`ing
   `{"attributes":{"color":"red"}}` (a *different* key, no `registrationNumber` in the body at all)
   **replaced the entire map** — the asset's attributes afterward were `{"color":"red"}` only,
   `registrationNumber` silently gone. This confirms both `withRegistrationNumber`/
   `withoutRegistrationNumber`'s "always start from the asset's complete current `attributes`" design
   and the attributes editor's "submit every row, not a delta" design are load-bearing, not
   theoretical caution.
6. **Discovery's `suggestedCategory` still isn't wired into the Profile step's category picker**
   (pre-existing Gotcha, unchanged this cycle — see that entry above) — now arguably more relevant
   than before, since the wizard's Profile step (category) comes right before a Connect step that may

   session (no real discoverable device on this network) — unchanged in kind from the pre-existing

## F-d — flight-controller-aware telemetry UI (docs/FC-INTEGRATIONS-PLAN.md F-d)

Built against the plan's own **frozen wire contract** ahead of/alongside the backend half
(F-a/F-b/F-c, other agents, `vision-domain`/`adapters/adapter-mavlink`/`vision-application`/
`vision-api`/`adapters/adapter-persistence`/`adapters/adapter-simulation` — outside this module's own
scope, not touched here) — delivers the frontend half of Tier-1's "RTH/failsafe banners" and
"telemetry-verified pre-flight checklist" over the existing telemetry pipe (`TelemetrySample`'s new
`flightState?`/`extra?`, `AssetAttention`'s new `flightMode?`/`armed?`/`failsafe?` — see the API
surface section above for the exact wire shapes).

**New**: `core/telemetry/flight-state-logic.ts` (+`.spec.ts`, 34 cases — see its own bullet above in
API surface/core for the full function list: `gpsFixLabel`/`gpsSeverity`/`flightBanner`/
`derivePreflight`), `features/fly/failsafe-banner.ts` (`<vision-failsafe-banner>`), `features/fly/preflight-checklist.ts`
(`<vision-preflight-checklist>`).

- **`features/fly/failsafe-banner.ts`** — a full-width strip laid over the video
  (`fly.html`'s new `.hud.hud-banner`, `z-index: 10` — above the ordinary HUD layer's `6` but below
  the two modals' `20`), kind-dependent class (`banner-failsafe` → `--live` red; `banner-rth`/
  `banner-landing` → `--warn` amber), B612 Mono uppercase. Deliberately dumb: one input
  (`banner: FlightBanner | null`), computed once in `fly.ts` (`failsafeBanner = computed(() =>
  flightBanner(telemetry.latest()))`) and passed straight through — the component owns no
  telemetry access of its own. **Geometric shift, not a z-index chase** (mirrors `.hud-map`'s own
  "BROKEN #1" fix, Gotchas above): `fly.css`'s `.cockpit.banner-active` class (bound to
  `failsafeBanner() !== null`) shifts `.hud-header`/`.hud-osd`/`.hud-map`/`.hud-ticker` down by the
  banner's own fixed `min-height: 2.2rem` rather than letting the banner paint over (and block
  clicks on) the header's own controls.
- **`features/fly/preflight-checklist.ts`** — a compact card (`✓`/`✕`/`—` glyphs, severity-colored)
  listing `derivePreflight`'s 5 rows verbatim, arming blockers rendered under the Armable row exactly
  as the FC reported them (`^(PreArm|Arm): …`, never paraphrased). Shown (`fly.html`'s new
  `.hud.hud-preflight`, bottom-left, clear of both `.hud-controls` and `.hud-secondary`) only when
  `!watchMode() && (telemetry.latest() === undefined || latest.flightState?.armed !== true)` — a
  ground-check card, gone once the FC confirms armed (the OSD chip bar is the live instrument from
  that point on).
- **`features/fly/fly-osd.ts`** — 5 new chips, each independently omitted (never fabricated) when its
  own field is absent: **ground speed** (`extra['groundspeedMps']`, one decimal, `X.Xm/s` — closes
  this component's own previously-documented "no speed reading" gap, doc comment rewritten in place
  rather than left stale), **mode** (`flightState.mode`, plain text), **GPS** (`gpsFixLabel` +
  satellite count, e.g. `"3D · 12 sat"`, colored by `gpsSeverity`), **armed/disarmed** (armed: mild
  green-ish, matching no other severity tier so it doesn't compete with `--live`/`--danger`;
  disarmed: dimmed via `opacity`, not colored — a grounded drone is the normal state, not a flag),
  **RSSI** (`rssiPercent` + `%`, plain chip, no severity tier — the plan didn't call for one and none
  of this app's other raw-signal readouts invents a threshold without a documented one).
- **`features/command/command-logic.ts`** — `AttentionReasonKind` gains **`failsafe`** (new top rank,
  above `battery-critical` — `REASON_RANK` renumbered `failsafe:6 … open-events:1`, kept internally
  consistent, not just appended) and **`gps-degraded`** (ranked between `battery-low` and
  `open-events`). `failsafeReason(asset)` fires only on `asset.failsafe === true` (never fabricated
  from `undefined`/`false`); `gpsDegradedReason(gpsFixType)` takes `gpsFixType` as an explicit
  parameter — **not** read off `AssetAttention` (the fleet-summary DTO doesn't carry GPS quality, see
  that interface's own doc comment) — reusing `flight-state-logic.ts#gpsSeverity` rather than
  re-deriving the tiers. `attentionReasons`/`buildEntityRows` both gained an optional trailing
  parameter (`gpsFixType?`/`gpsFixTypeByAssetId?: ReadonlyMap<string, number>`) so every existing call
  site (`asset-panel.ts`) keeps compiling unchanged; `command.ts`'s own `entityRows` computed now
  builds that map from `mapStore.markers()` (`FleetMarker.gpsFixType`) and threads it through — an
  asset with no live marker simply never triggers that one reason, the same "unknown, not fabricated"
  posture as everywhere else in this module.
- **`core/map/map-logic.ts#FleetMarker`** gained `flightMode?`/`armed?`/`failsafe?`/`gpsFixType?`,
  sourced in `buildMarker` from the streaming asset's own latest telemetry sample's `flightState` —
  the same place `headingDegrees`/`batteryPercent` already come from, **not** `AssetSummary`
  (`buildMarker`'s own input, which carries none of this) and deliberately a *different* derivation
  from `AssetAttention`'s own `flightMode`/`armed`/`failsafe` (fleet-summary-level; see that
  interface's doc comment) — this marker-level set exists for `shared/map/fleet-map/fleet-map.ts`'s
  popup and `features/command/asset-panel.ts`'s Status tab, both of which only ever have a
  `FleetMarker` in hand. Absent entirely for the `offline` bucket (no live telemetry poller).
- **`features/command/asset-panel.ts`/`.html`** — Status tab gained Mode/Armed/GPS facts, preferring
  the live `marker()` (fresher) and falling back to the fleet-summary `asset()` fields for
  Mode/Armed (GPS has no such fallback — marker-only, `AssetAttention` doesn't carry it); Armed
  renders as plain text (`'Armed'`/`'Disarmed'`/`'—'`), deliberately uncolored — armed isn't itself a
  severity. GPS text colors `.gps-warn-text`/`.gps-critical-text` (new, `asset-panel.css`) rather than
  reusing the existing `.stale-text` (red-only) — one primary signal color per severity tier, amber
  vs. red kept distinct. `reasons` now threads `marker()?.gpsFixType` into `attentionReasons`.
- **`features/command/command.html`/`.css`** — rail row gained a compact **`FS`** chip
  (`.fs-chip`, `--live` red via `color-mix`, this app's existing precedent for a translucent tinted
  background — `app.css`) shown when `row.asset.failsafe === true`, distinct from the generic
  `.severity-chip` (`--danger`-based) so a genuine failsafe always reads as more urgent than any other
  critical reason at a glance.
- **`features/wall/wall-tile.ts`** — a small uppercase mode badge in the tile footer
  (`.mode-chip`, plain neutral by default), red (`--live`) when `flightState.failsafe === true`;
  omitted entirely with no `flightState` yet (a device with no MAVLink-carrying telemetry link still
  shows nothing, never a fabricated "—" badge cluttering every tile).
- **`shared/map/fleet-map/fleet-map.ts`** — popup gained a `Mode …` line when `marker.flightMode` is
  present, inline-styled `--live` red + bold when `marker.failsafe === true` (Leaflet popups are raw
  HTML strings, not Angular templates — a CSS class defined in this component's own `fleet-map.css`
  wouldn't reach it any more directly than the inline style does; `escapeHtml` still guards the mode
  string like every other user-controlled popup field here).

**Poka-yoke, verified across every piece above**: no fake data anywhere — every absent field renders
`'—'`/no chip/no row, never a fabricated `'ok'`/green tick; `--live` red is used *only* for a genuine
failsafe (never a generic "critical" severity, which stays on `--danger`); banners/badges state what
the *aircraft* is doing (`'FAILSAFE — RETURNING TO HOME'`), never an instruction to the operator.

**Tests**: 809/809 passing across 43 files (`flight-state-logic.spec.ts` alone: 34 cases covering
`gpsFixLabel`/`gpsSeverity`/`flightBanner`/every `derivePreflight` row; `command-logic.spec.ts`/
`map-logic.spec.ts` both extended for the new reason kinds/marker fields). **One pre-existing,
unrelated bug fixed as a build-gate blocker**: `shared/map/fleet-plan-dialog/flight-plan-logic.spec.ts`
imported `'./flight-plan-logic'` (same directory) instead of `'../flight-plan-logic'` (one level
up, where the file actually lives — `flight-plan-dialog.ts` in the same folder already used the
correct relative path) — a stale reference from an earlier `fleet-plan-dialog/` directory reorg,
confirmed pre-existing via `git log`/`git status` (untouched by this or any other in-flight task),
and confirmed to break `ng test`/`ng build` for the **entire** app, not just that one file — fixed as
a one-line import-path correction since it made this task's own build gate unverifiable otherwise.

**Build**: `ng build` compiles clean — **333.25 kB raw / 94.02 kB transfer initial** (still inside the
360 kB error budget; the pre-existing 300 kB warning overage, carried since U2's CDK addition, grows
by this cycle's own share — a precise before/after delta wasn't captured, since no clean pre-task
build was taken per the reactor-wide "don't build while another agent holds modules red" rule with
three backend modules in flight this batch). `fly`/`command`/`wall` are all lazy chunks (idle-preloaded,
per `core/idle-preload.ts`) — none of this cycle's own code (`flight-state-logic.ts`,
`failsafe-banner.ts`, `preflight-checklist.ts`) is reachable from the eager initial bundle; grep-verified
absent from the initial chunk list the same way every past cycle in this file has checked.

## I-b "Listen for drones" onboarding + F-e ArduPilot diagnostics surfaces (docs/DRONE-INFRA-PLAN.md I-b, docs/FC-INTEGRATIONS-PLAN.md F-e)

Both built against each plan's own frozen contract while the backend halves (`MavlinkHeartbeatScanner`
in `adapter-mavlink`, F-e's `WIND`/`VIBRATION`/`EKF_STATUS_REPORT`/`RANGEFINDER`/`MISSION_CURRENT`
decode) landed concurrently in the same working tree — **the actual landed backend code was read
afterward, read-only, to verify this frontend's own assumptions against it** (not to write against
it directly; this module's file scope stayed `vision-web/**` only): `MavlinkHeartbeatScanner#toDiscoveredDevice`
confirms the exact `details` map shape this task assumed (`details["firmware"]` — literal `"unknown"`
string, never absent; `details["mavType"]`; `details["sysid"]`; `details["claimed"] = "true"` +
`details["claimedBy"]` only once a vehicle is claimed) and the exact name format
(`"ArduPilot quadcopter (sysid 7)"`) — both match this section's own `drone-scan-logic.ts` below
verbatim, no rework needed. One wire-shape subtlety worth recording: `StreamDescriptor.options`
(the domain-level `sysid` pin) is **not** flattened by `dto.DiscoveredDeviceResponse` — that DTO
only ever flattens `protocol`/`uri` from `suggestedStream`, never its `options` map (verified by
reading `DiscoveredDeviceResponse.java`, unmodified by either concurrent backend task) — which is
exactly why the backend redundantly duplicates the sysid into `details["sysid"]` too, and why
`drone-scan-logic.ts#prefillFromVehicle` reads it from there rather than expecting it on
`DiscoveredDevice` directly.

### Part 1 — I-b: `features/onboarding/**`'s fourth Connect-step tile

- **New `features/onboarding/drone-scan-logic.ts`** (+`.spec.ts`, 18 cases) — pure, Angular-free,
  mirrors `onboarding-logic.ts`'s own split: `MAVLINK_DISCOVERY_METHOD` (`"mavlink"`),
  `buildMavlinkScanRequest(timeoutMs?)` (→ `ScanRequest` restricted to `methods: ['mavlink']`; no
  `timeoutMs` sent unless given — the scanner self-time-boxes ~5-10s, so unlike the general
  "Discover on network" method there is no timeout picker for this one), `isClaimedVehicle`
  (`details['claimed'] === 'true'`), `vehicleSysid` (`details['sysid']`), `vehicleDetailChips`
  (firmware + sysid only — a deliberately narrower pair than the general Discover table's
  generic `detailPairs` dump of every `details` entry, per the plan's own "details chips (firmware,
  sysid)" wording; `mavType`/`claimedBy` ride in `details` unused by this UI), and
  `prefillFromVehicle` (→ protocol/uri/`{sysid}` options/`suggestedCategory`, falling back to
  `"mavlink"`/`candidate.address` the same way `useCandidate` already falls back for the general
  Discover path). `ScanRequest.methods`/`DiscoveredDevice.details` already existed in `models.ts`
  pre-task (no wire-model change needed) — verified before writing any of the above, not assumed.
- **`onboarding-logic.ts`**: `ConnectMethod` gains a fourth member, `'listen'` — behaves exactly like
  `'discover'` in every existing gate (`canAdvanceFromConnect`'s `'listen'` case returns `false`,
  same as `'discover'`; `nextStep`/`prevStep` needed no change at all, since their own `method ===
  'simulate' ? … : …` ternary already treats every non-simulate method identically). Picking an
  unclaimed vehicle flips the method to `'register'` with the form prefilled — the same
  "candidate → register" pivot `'discover'`'s own "Use" button already uses, not a new step-machine
  state. 3 new `onboarding-logic.spec.ts` cases cover the `'listen'` variant through
  `nextStep`/`canAdvanceFromConnect`/`canAdvanceFromTest`.
- **`onboarding-store.ts`**: `droneScanning`/`droneScanResult` signals + `scanForDrones()` (calls
  `api.scan(buildMavlinkScanRequest())`, no timeout picker) + `useDroneVehicle(candidate)` (a
  no-op for an already-claimed candidate — defensive, since the UI never offers the action for one
  anyway; otherwise mirrors `useCandidate` plus filling `category` from `suggestedCategory` when the
  Profile step's own category field is still blank — this is the one path in this wizard that closes
  the previously-documented "discovery's `suggestedCategory` isn't wired into the Profile step's
  category picker" Gotcha, for `listen` specifically, not the general `discover` method).
- **`onboarding.ts`/`.html`/`.css`**: a fourth `.method-tile` ("Listen for drones", its own
  `.glyph-listen` — concentric rings, distinct from `.glyph-discover`'s dashed ring) alongside the
  original three; a new `@case ('listen')` results view — one `<li class="vehicle-row">` per heard
  vehicle (name, `vehicleDetailChips`' firmware/sysid chips, a `Use` button), **not** the general
  Discover method's `<table>` (fewer columns fit this scan's narrower "drone only" purpose better);
  a claimed vehicle renders `.vehicle-row.claimed` (dimmed, `opacity: 0.6`) with `Use` disabled and
  an inline `.disabled-reason` "Already registered" (the U-a2 poka-yoke idiom this app already uses
  everywhere else a disabled control needs its reason told inline, not just in a tooltip) — never
  hidden, so the scan's own "it heard this vehicle" proof stays visible. Empty scan (`result.devices
  .length === 0`): "No drones heard on UDP 14550 — check the link recipes in infra/edge/." + a
  rescan button, per the plan's own exact wording. Loading state mirrors the wizard's existing style
  (`store.droneScanning()` → "Listening…" button label + an `.empty`/`.faint` "Listening for
  heartbeats…" placeholder, same idiom as every other async step in this wizard). "Allow
  cancel-by-navigation" needed no explicit `AbortController`: leaving `/add-source` destroys the
  per-route-provided `OnboardingStore` (`providers: [OnboardingStore]` on `OnboardingPage`), so an
  in-flight scan's eventual response simply has nowhere left to land.
- **`CONNECT_METHOD_LABELS`**/`connectionSummary`'s exhaustive switches both gained the `'listen'`
  case (label `'Listen for drones'`; summary groups with `'register'`/`'discover'` — in practice
  never reached, since picking a vehicle always flips the method to `'register'` first, identical
  to how `'discover'`'s own case in that switch is already unreachable in practice).

### Part 2 — F-e: diagnostics rows, reused by Fly and Command

- **New `core/telemetry/flight-state-logic.ts` additions** (+22 new spec cases, same file/spec
  `F-d` already owns — no new file, this is a natural extension of the existing flight-state pure
  logic): `ekfSeverity(variance)` (ok `< 0.5`, warn `0.5–1.0` inclusive, bad `> 1.0`),
  `vibeSeverity(ms2)` (ok `< 30`, warn `30–60` inclusive, bad `> 60`), `DiagnosticRow`/
  `DiagnosticSeverity` types, and `deriveDiagnostics(extra: Record<string, number> | undefined)` —
  **takes the raw `extra` map directly, not a whole `TelemetrySample`**, specifically so both
  consumers below can feed it whatever `extra` they actually have in hand (a live sample's own
  `extra` for Fly, a `FleetMarker`'s passed-through `extra` for Command) without either one needing
  a second telemetry poller. Five possible rows, fixed order, each independently omitted (not
  present with a fabricated value) when its own frozen `extra` key(s) are absent: **wind**
  (`windSpeedMps` + optional `windDirectionDegrees`, no severity — F-e defines none), **vibration**
  (any of `vibeXMs2`/`vibeYMs2`/`vibeZMs2` present; only the present axes are shown, the *worst*
  one's own value governs the row's severity), **EKF** (any of `ekfVelocityVariance`/
  `ekfPosHorizVariance`/`ekfPosVertVariance`/`ekfCompassVariance`; the worst one governs both the
  severity and which one is named in the value string, e.g. `"compass 1.23"` — never averaged,
  never silently picking the first one present), **rangefinder** (`rangefinderDistanceM`, no
  severity), **mission** (`missionSeq` → `"Waypoint <n>"`, no severity).
- **`core/map/map-logic.ts#FleetMarker`** gained `extra?: Record<string, number>`, sourced in
  `buildMarker` from the same latest-sample the existing `flightMode`/`armed`/etc. fields already
  read (`latest?.extra`, verbatim passthrough — no new derivation) — this is what lets
  `asset-panel.ts`'s Status tab call `deriveDiagnostics` without a second `TelemetryStore` poller,
  the identical reuse rationale F-d's own `flightMode`/`armed`/`failsafe`/`gpsFixType` additions
  already established for this interface. Absent entirely for the `offline` bucket, same rule as
  every other live-telemetry-sourced field here. 3 new `map-logic.spec.ts` cases.
- **`features/fly/diagnostics-card.ts`** (new, `<vision-diagnostics-card>`) — a compact,
  collapsible HUD card (own internal `expanded` signal, default open; a header button toggles it),
  `rows: readonly DiagnosticRow[]` its only input, deliberately dumb like `FailsafeBanner`/
  `PreflightChecklist` — issues no HTTP, holds no store. Hidden entirely with no rows, via the same
  belt-and-suspenders double-guard `FailsafeBanner` already uses (an internal `@if (rows().length >
  0)` *and* `fly.html`'s own `@if (diagnosticsRows().length > 0)` around the `<vision-diagnostics-
  card>` tag). Severity colors reuse this app's existing tokens: plain default for `'ok'`/no-severity
  rows, `--warn` amber, `--danger` red — **never `--live`**, which this app reserves exclusively for
  a genuine failsafe (see `FailsafeBanner`'s own doc comment; F-e's own bad-vibration/bad-EKF rows
  are "attention", not "the aircraft is failsafing"). `fly.ts#diagnosticsRows = computed(() =>
  deriveDiagnostics(telemetry.latest()?.extra))`; `fly.html`'s new `.hud.hud-diagnostics` sits at
  `right: 0.75rem; bottom: 5.7rem` (`fly.css`) — the right-column mirror of `.hud-preflight`'s own
  fixed left-column offset above `.hud-controls`, clear of `.hud-detections` below and `.hud-map`
  above at this app's ≥1280px viewport floor (an approximate, not computed, offset — same caveat
  `.hud-preflight`'s own comment already carries).
- **`features/fly/fly-osd.ts`** gained a wind chip — `extra['windSpeedMps']` (one decimal, `X.Xm/s`)
  plus an arrow glyph (`➤`) rotated via an inline `[style.transform]="'rotate(' + deg + 'deg)'"` to
  `extra['windDirectionDegrees']` when that key is also present; the speed alone still renders
  without a direction. A second, compact rendering of the same wind data the Diagnostics card's own
  row shows — not a duplicate derivation, no severity tier (F-e defines none for wind).
- **`features/command/asset-panel.ts`/`.html`/`.css`** — Status tab's `dl.facts` gained the same
  `deriveDiagnostics` rows, appended after the existing Mode/Armed/GPS facts, fed from
  `marker()?.extra` (never a second poller — see `FleetMarker#extra`'s own doc comment above). New
  `.diag-warn-text`/`.diag-bad-text` CSS (`color: var(--warn)`/`var(--danger)`) rather than reusing
  `.gps-warn-text`/`.gps-critical-text` verbatim — those two are functionally identical color rules,
  but keeping a separate, non-GPS-named pair avoids a non-GPS row's class name reading as
  GPS-specific to a future reader.
- **No new component specs** for `DiagnosticsCard`/the onboarding wizard's `'listen'` sub-view —
  matches this codebase's own standing precedent (`FailsafeBanner`/`PreflightChecklist`/
  `AssetPanel`/`OnboardingPage` all have none either; only the pure `*-logic.ts` layer is
  unit-tested, component behavior is covered by the full production build + this doc's own
  description, per the established convention this file's own "Tests" sections have followed since
  U2).

### Tests / build

**854/854 passing across 44 files** (up from 809/43 — +45: 18 `drone-scan-logic.spec.ts` + 22 new
`flight-state-logic.spec.ts` cases (`ekfSeverity`/`vibeSeverity`/`deriveDiagnostics`) + 3 new
`map-logic.spec.ts` cases (`FleetMarker#extra`) + 2 new `onboarding-logic.spec.ts` cases (the
`'listen'` `ConnectMethod`'s own never-advances-directly/canAdvanceFromTest coverage; two more
pre-existing cases were extended in place to also assert `'listen'`, not counted as new). `npx tsc
--noEmit` clean on both `tsconfig.app.json`/`tsconfig.spec.json`.

**Build**: `ng build` (production) compiles clean — **333.25 kB raw / 94.02 kB transfer initial**,
**unchanged** from F-d's own recorded number — grep-verified none of this cycle's new identifiers
(`deriveDiagnostics`/`DiagnosticsCard`/`drone-scan-logic`) or new user-facing strings ("Listen for
drones", "No drones heard on UDP 14550", "Diagnostics") reach either eager initial chunk, only the
`onboarding`/`fly` lazy chunks (**`onboarding`: 58.15 kB raw / 14.62 kB transfer**, up from U-d's
52.73/13.62 — the fourth Connect tile + `drone-scan-logic.ts` + the vehicle-list template/CSS;
**`fly`: 37.42 kB raw / 9.38 kB transfer** — this cycle's own Diagnostics card + OSD wind chip;
neither `fly`'s nor `command`'s pre-this-cycle lazy-chunk size was individually recorded in this
file's own F-d entry to diff against, only the initial-total number was, so only the current
absolute figures are recorded here). Still comfortably inside the 360 kB error budget; the
pre-existing 300 kB warning overage (carried since U2's CDK addition) is unchanged, since the
initial total itself didn't move at all this cycle.

## Q2/Q3 — ops quick wins: Undo toast, replay strip cap, composite box hues (docs/OPS-CORE-PLAN.md §Q)

Scope: `vision-web/**` only (Wave A, parallel with G-a/R-a's own Java-side work; Q1's event→replay
deep link is a later agent's — this batch left `features/replay/replay.ts`'s own structure alone
beyond the strip cap, and never touched `features/onboarding/**`/`core/telemetry/flight-state-logic.ts`).
No backend changes.

**Q2 — 10s Undo toast, closing U-a2 item 3b's own documented gap.** New `shared/ui/undo-toast.service.ts`/
`undo-toast.ts` (`UndoToastService`/`<vision-undo-toast>`) — see the API-surface bullet above (next to
`ToastService`) for the full shape: one toast at a time, 10s default, commit-on-replace/undo-only-cancels,
a CSS-animated progress bar, `Escape`-dismissable, mounted at the app root (`app.html`) **bottom-left**
(checked every existing `position: fixed`/`.hud-*` layout in the app first — `ToastHost` already owns
bottom-right, `features/fly/fly.css#.hud-controls` owns bottom-center at every supported viewport).
Wired into `features/devices/devices.ts`/`features/asset-detail/asset-detail.ts`'s existing `archiveAssetNow`/
`archiveDeviceNow` (previously `ToastService.ok(text, {label:'Undo', onClick})`) **and** newly into
device `deactivate` via new `deactivateDeviceNow` methods on both pages (previously silent-immediacy —
a plain, action-less `FleetStore.setDeviceState` confirmation toast). `.danger-action` semantics
(the Archive button itself) are untouched — only what happens *after* the click changed. Asset-level
`activate`/`deactivate` are not reachable from any UI surface (U-a2 item 2 dropped them from every
operator-facing asset surface), so there was nothing to wire there; the explicit `restore`/`activate`
kebab entries are deliberately unchanged (re-activating isn't "silent immediacy" in the sense this item
means). `zone-delete` (the plan's third named flow) doesn't exist yet — G-c's zones UI lands in Wave B —
so `showUndo` is written generically enough for that agent to reuse verbatim, not wired to anything itself.

**Q3a — replay event strip cap.** `replay-logic.ts#capDetectionBuckets(buckets, cap = DETECTION_STRIP_CAP
= 200)` — a pure slice (no virtualization library) keeping only the latest 200 buckets plus the pre-cap
`totalCount`; `replay.ts` applies it over the existing `bucketDetections` output, and `replay.html` shows
a quiet "Showing latest N of totalCount markers" line under the scrub bar only when something was
actually trimmed. Rarely engages today — `DEFAULT_DETECTION_BUCKETS` (120) already sits under the 200
cap — kept as a real, tested backstop regardless rather than trusting bucket-count tuning alone forever.

**Q3b — composite box hues.** `shared/player/detection-overlay-logic.ts` gains `detectionModelKey(detection)`
(label-prefix extraction — deliberately not `Detection.modelId`, which is uniform across an entire
composite batch on the wire and so can't distinguish members within one; see that function's own doc
comment), `modelHue(modelKey, alphaPercent?)` (hash → hue, fixed saturation/lightness; `DEFAULT_MODEL_KEY`
always returns the byte-identical original box color, so a single-model stream — whichever model —
sees zero change), and `distinctModelKeys(detections)`. `shared/player/player.ts#drawBox` strokes/fills
every box via `modelHue` instead of the old hardcoded literals; a top-right legend chip (model → color
swatch) renders only once `showModelLegend` (≥2 distinct keys in the batch currently on screen,
`overlayResult`/`overlayModelKeys`) is true.

**Tests**: 854/854 → **884/884, 44 → 45 files** (+30: 11 new `shared/ui/undo-toast.service.spec.ts`,
+13 `shared/player/detection-overlay-logic.spec.ts` (`detectionModelKey`/`modelHue`/`distinctModelKeys`),
+6 `features/replay/replay-logic.spec.ts` (`capDetectionBuckets`)). `ng test --watch=false` (this is the
one build gate that must go through the Angular CLI's own test builder, not raw `npx vitest run` — see
this file's own Build/test line up top) green throughout. `npx tsc` clean via `ng build`.

**Build**: `ng build` (production) compiles clean — **336.70 kB raw / 94.87 kB transfer initial**, up
+3.45 kB/+0.85 kB from the prior recorded 333.25/94.02 — almost entirely `<vision-undo-toast>` itself
(mounted eagerly at the app root, same as `<vision-toast-host>`); `detection-overlay-logic.ts`'s new
exports only ever reach `player.ts`, which stays lazy-chunk-only as before. **The 300 kB initial-bundle
warning is pre-existing, verified independently of this task**: a clean `git worktree add … HEAD` build
(no uncommitted changes from this or any other concurrent agent) already reported **333.25 kB raw**,
33.25 kB over budget, before this batch's own +3.45 kB — carried since U2's CDK addition (see F-d's own
entry above), not something this task introduced or is in scope to fix. Still comfortably inside the
360 kB error budget (`maximumError`) either way; build exit code 0.

Files touched: `shared/ui/undo-toast.service.ts`/`.spec.ts`, `shared/ui/undo-toast.ts` (new);
`app.ts`/`app.html` (mount); `features/devices/devices.ts`, `features/asset-detail/asset-detail.ts`
(archive/deactivate wiring only — no template changes, `.danger-action` markup untouched);
`features/replay/replay-logic.ts`/`.spec.ts`, `features/replay/replay.ts`, `features/replay/replay.html`;

## G-c / R-c / W / Q1 — geofence zones UI, recording video + clip export, weather go/no-go chip, event→replay deep link (docs/OPS-CORE-PLAN.md)

Scope: `vision-web/**` only, built against the plan's own frozen wire contracts (§G, §R) — the
parallel Java-side agents (G-a/G-b, R-a/R-b) had already landed `GeofenceController`,
`UsageTimelineController#recording`, `GeofenceZone`/`GeofenceZoneResponse`/`UsageRecordingResponse`,
and `MediamtxStreamPublisher#playbackUrl` by the time this batch started (verified by reading the
actual Java source directly, not assumed from the plan text alone) — no backend stub-guessing was
needed anywhere in this batch.

**G-c — geofence zones UI.** New `core/geofence/**` (`geofence-logic.ts`, `geofence-store.ts` +
both specs), the zones layer on `shared/map/fleet-map/**`/`shared/map/live-map/**`, the new
`geofence-breach` top-rank attention reason in `command-logic.ts`, and Command's own Zones
panel/draw dialog (`features/command/zones-panel.ts`, `features/command/geofence-zone-dialog.ts`) —
full write-up in the dedicated `core/geofence/**` section above (and the Command/Fly sections'
own new bullets). The breach-toast half lands in `shared/ui/notification-bell.ts` (see that
component's own section above).

**W — weather go/no-go chip.** New `core/weather/**` (`weather-logic.ts`, `weather-store.ts` +
both specs) and `shared/ui/weather-chip.ts`, wired into Command's topbar and Fly's OSD — full
write-up in the dedicated `core/weather/**` section above.

**R-c — recording video pane + clip export.** `features/replay/replay-logic.ts` gained the
video-sync/clip-window/deep-link-offset pure helpers; `replay.ts`/`.html`/`.css` gained the video
pane, the guarded two-way scrub↔video sync, and "Download clip" — full write-up in the Replay
section above.

**Q1 — event→replay deep link.** `core/events/events-logic.ts#findCoveringUsage`/
`resolveReplayDeepLink` (+specs), a new flat `/replay` route sharing `ReplayPage`, and async
`navigate`/`openEvent` methods in `shared/ui/notification-bell.ts`/`features/wall/wall.ts` — full
write-up in the Replay/notification-bell sections above.

### Deviations from the task brief, with reasons

- **The deep link this app actually builds is `/replay?asset=<id>&usage=<id>&t=<offsetMs>`**, not
  the plan's own literal shorthand `/replay?usage=<id>&t=<offsetMs>` (no `asset`). `ReplayPage`
  needs an asset id to fetch device names/telemetry-device grouping (`getAsset(assetId)`); every
  call site that builds this link (`NotificationBell.navigate`, `WallPage.openEvent`) already has
  `event.assetId` in hand at the exact moment it resolves the deep link (no extra lookup), so
  including it costs nothing and avoids a degraded "no device names" rendering that would otherwise
  need its own fallback path. `assetId` is still genuinely *optional* on the flat route (a
  hand-typed URL omitting it degrades gracefully — see `ReplayPage`'s own class doc comment) for
  robustness, but in practice this app never constructs a link without it.
- **Zones management is a modal overlay (`<vision-zones-panel>`), not a third `commandGridColumns`
  grid track.** The plan says "a Zones management panel in Command" without pinning the exact
  layout; a third always-reserved side panel would mean re-touching `command-logic.ts#commandGridColumns`'s
  own tested 2×3 grid arithmetic for a feature used occasionally (draw/rename/delete zones), not
  browsed continuously the way the entity rail/asset panel are — the same "modal, not embedded"
  reasoning `shared/map/fleet-plan-dialog/flight-plan-dialog.ts` already established for flight-plan
  authoring.
- **The zone-draw dialog is create-only** — there is no "Edit boundary" entry on an existing zone
  (only rename/enable-disable/delete, all in the Zones panel's own list). `GeofenceStore.redraw()`
  exists and is fully wired to the API, ready for a future cycle to add that entry; not adding the
  UI for it this batch was a scope call (the plan's own poka-yoke bullets only ask for the draw-new
  flow's own validations, not an edit flow).
- **Command's weather chip always uses the plan's own 10 m/s default limit**, never a specific
  asset's `windLimitMps` override — `AssetAttention` (the fleet-summary row `CommandPage` already
  polls) carries no `attributes` field at all to read one from, and fetching a second per-asset
  lookup just for this one advisory chip was judged disproportionate; Fly's chip (scoped to one
  selected asset, whose `AssetDetails.attributes` this app already has) is the one that honors it.
- **`shared/map/fleet-map/fleet-map.ts`'s own event-marker popup "Details" button was not wired to
  Q1's deep link** — still asset-only, a deliberate scoping call (raw-HTML popups driven by one
  delegated click listener aren't a natural place to host an async `VisionApi` lookup + router
  navigation) documented on that component's own class doc comment and the MODULE.md bullet it
  used to describe as an "unbuildable" gap (now closed elsewhere, just not wired in here).

### Tests

**884/884 → 994/994, 45 → 49 files** (+110 cases, +4 new spec files: `core/geofence/geofence-logic.spec.ts`,
`core/geofence/geofence-store.spec.ts`, `core/weather/weather-logic.spec.ts`,
`core/weather/weather-store.spec.ts`; plus new cases folded into `core/api/vision-api.spec.ts`
(the 4 geofence CRUD calls + `usageRecording`), `features/command/command-logic.spec.ts`
(`geofence-breach` reason + `buildEntityRows`'s new optional argument), `features/replay/replay-logic.spec.ts`
(video-sync/clip-window/`buildClipDownloadUrl`/deep-link-offset helpers), and `core/events/events-logic.spec.ts`
(`findCoveringUsage`/`resolveReplayDeepLink`)). `WeatherStore`'s own spec mocks `Date.now()` directly
(`vi.spyOn(Date, 'now')`) rather than `vi.useFakeTimers()` — a fake-timer `setTimeout`-based flush
helper never fires without explicit `vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync` calls threaded through every
`await`, and this store's own cache logic only ever reads `Date.now()`, never sets a timer itself,
so controlling the clock directly with real timers doing the actual microtask flushing is simpler
and was the first approach tried after the fake-timer version hung every test at its 5s timeout.
`npm run test:ci` (`ng test --watch=false`) green throughout.

### Build

`ng build --configuration production`: **339.76 kB raw / 95.94 kB transfer initial** — up

dialog/video pane are reachable only from the `command`/`fly`/`replay` route chunks. **The 300 kB
initial-bundle warning is pre-existing** (336.70 kB before this batch, itself already 33.25 kB over
budget per the Q2/Q3 Status entry) — still comfortably inside the 360 kB error budget; build exit
code 0.

## I-e Stage 1, wave 3 — "Bring home" UI (docs/DRONE-INFRA-PLAN.md I-e Stage 1's frozen contract)

The RX-only doctrine's one deliberate, guarded exception, staged: a single command,
`MAV_CMD_DO_SET_MODE → RTL`, reachable from exactly two places (Fly's cockpit HUD, Command's
`AssetPanel`), always behind a mandatory confirm dialog, never optimistic. Wave 1
(`FlightCommandPort`/`MavlinkFlightCommander`, `vision-domain`/`adapter-mavlink`) and wave 2
(`POST /api/assets/{assetId}/return-home`, `FlightCommandController`/`FlightCommandService`,
`vision-api`/`vision-application`) landed in parallel, outside this module's own scope — this wave
was coded against the plan's own frozen wire contract (`202 {result: "ACCEPTED"|"NO_ACK"}` / `404`
/ `409 {message}`) and only cross-checked against the landed backend DTO/controller afterward,
read-only, to confirm the assumption (`ReturnHomeResponse(String result)`,
`POST /api/assets/{id}/return-home`, `@ResponseStatus(HttpStatus.ACCEPTED)`) — an exact match, no
rework needed.

**New**: `shared/ui/confirm-dialog.ts`/`.html`/`.css`, `shared/ui/return-home-button.ts`/`.html`/`.css`,
`shared/ui/return-home-button-logic.ts`(+`.spec.ts`). **Reshaped**: `core/api/models.ts`
(`ReturnHomeResult`/`ReturnHomeResponse`), `core/api/vision-api.ts`(+`.spec.ts` — `returnHome(assetId)`),
`core/telemetry/flight-state-logic.ts`(+`.spec.ts` — `canCommandReturnHome`), `core/map/map-logic.ts`
(+`.spec.ts` — `FleetMarker.firmware`), `core/toast.service.ts`(+`.spec.ts` — the new `warning` kind
+ `warn()`), `shared/ui/toast-host.ts` (`.toast.warning` CSS), `features/fly/fly.ts`/`.html`,
`features/command/asset-panel.ts`/`.html`.

- **`core/telemetry/flight-state-logic.ts#canCommandReturnHome(firmware, ageSeconds)`** — the one
  gate, per the plan: `firmware === 'ardupilot'` (INAV masquerades as ArduPilot on the wire — this
  one check is deliberately the *complete* gate, Betaflight/unknown firmware never reports that
  string) **and** telemetry is fresh, reusing `isStale`/`STALE_AFTER_SECONDS` (`core/telemetry/telemetry-logic.ts`)
  directly rather than a second "fresh enough to command" constant. Takes plain `firmware`/`ageSeconds`
  values, not a whole `TelemetrySample` — mirrors `deriveDiagnostics`'s own reasoning: this gate's
  two call sites already have the answer in different shapes (Fly's `TelemetryStore.latest()` sample
  + a `nowMs` read; Command's `FleetMarker`, whose `sampleAgeSeconds` is already precomputed and
  carries no nested `flightState` at all). **Note**: `ageSeconds !== undefined` is checked explicitly
  ahead of `isStale` — `isStale(undefined)` itself returns `false` (its own "unknown, don't assume
  bad" contract), but "no telemetry sample at all" must never read as "fresh enough to command"; the
  two absent-data rules diverge on purpose here (caught by a spec case, not just reasoned about).
- **`core/map/map-logic.ts#FleetMarker` gained `firmware?: string`**, sourced in `buildMarker` from
  the same place `flightMode`/`armed`/`failsafe`/`gpsFixType` already are (the streaming asset's
  latest sample's own `flightState`) — the identical F-d/F-e reuse rationale, needed here because
  `AssetAttention` (the fleet-summary DTO `AssetPanel`'s other inputs come from) carries no firmware
  field at all; this marker-level field is Command's only source.
- **`shared/ui/confirm-dialog.ts`** (`<vision-confirm-dialog>`, new) — the first reusable modal
  confirm in `shared/ui/`: a true `position: fixed` backdrop (`z-index: 150`, mirrors
  `shared/map/fleet-plan-dialog/flight-plan-dialog.ts`'s/`features/command/geofence-zone-dialog.ts`'s
  own convention), not Fly's page-local `.hud-confirm`/`.inline-confirm-card` scrim — needed because
  this dialog has to render correctly from two different host layouts (Fly's full-bleed cockpit,
  Command's docked side panel), neither of which it can lean on. `message`/`confirmLabel`/
  `cancelLabel`/`busy` inputs, `confirmed`/`cancelled` outputs. Deliberately no backdrop-click or
  `Escape` dismissal — every close is an explicit button click, per the plan's own poka-yoke wording
  ("never skippable").
- **`shared/ui/return-home-button.ts`** (`<vision-return-home-button>`, new) — the shared "one
  component, two call sites" button (the same precedent `shared/ui/weather-chip.ts`/
  `shared/player/detections-strip.ts` established), reused verbatim by `FlyPage`/`AssetPanel`.
  Unlike `weather-chip.ts` (host-provided store, no HTTP of its own), this component owns its HTTP
  call and toast directly — `VisionApi`/`ToastService` are both `providedIn: 'root'`, so no
  page-scoped `providers` entry is needed, and there's no shared store a one-shot command like this
  could sensibly live behind. Inputs: `assetId`/`assetDisplayName` (the confirm text —
  `"Command <name> to return home?"`), `canCommand` (the host's own `canCommandReturnHome` verdict —
  gating is the host's job, this component only renders it, hidden entirely when `false`), `compact`
  (mirrors `<vision-player [compact]>`'s naming — both call sites pass `true`, a small `.btn.danger.small`).
  `:host { display: contents }` (mirrors the dialog components' own convention) so the button lands
  as a plain sibling in whatever flex row the host places it in (`fly.html`'s `.hud-header`,
  `asset-panel.html`'s `.panel-actions`), inheriting each row's own button sizing for free. Click →
  `confirmOpen` → `<vision-confirm-dialog>` → on confirm, `busy=true` (disables both dialog buttons
  and the trigger button itself — no double-fire, a plain signal, not a state machine; this is a
  single one-shot request, not a saga with retries the way `player-recovery.ts`'s reconnect logic
  is) → `VisionApi.returnHome(assetId)` → `returnHomeToastFor(result)`'s pure verdict (`shared/ui/return-home-button-logic.ts`,
  new, unit-tested — `ACCEPTED` → `ToastService.ok('Return home commanded')`, `NO_ACK` →
  `ToastService.warn('Command sent — no acknowledgement from aircraft')`) or, on a rejected promise,
  `ToastService.error(describeHttpError(error))` (`core/api-error.ts`'s existing switch already
  surfaces a `409`'s `message` verbatim and a network failure's status-0 case generically — no new
  error decoding needed here). **No optimistic UI, ever** (the plan's own wording): the button's job
  ends the moment the request settles; the aircraft's actual mode change arrives later over ordinary
  telemetry (`flightState.mode` → an RTL-family value), which `flightBanner`/`<vision-failsafe-banner>`
  already turns into "Return to home active" on its own, no wiring needed from this button.
- **`core/toast.service.ts` gained a `warning` kind + `warn(text)`** (no `action`, unlike `ok`/`notify` —
  there's nothing useful to click from a NO_ACK toast) — mirrors E-b's own precedent for adding
  `notification` (own `DISMISS_AFTER_MS` entry, 7s, between `notification`'s 6s and `error`'s 9s;
  own `.toast.warning` CSS in `shared/ui/toast-host.ts`, the app's existing `--warn` amber, distinct
  from `.error`'s red — a NO_ACK command genuinely went out, it isn't a failure).
- **Fly cockpit (`fly.ts`/`.html`)**: `canBringHome` computed (mirrors `preflightItems`'s own
  "re-derives whenever the tracked sample changes, not a continuously-ticking clock" convention) →
  `<vision-return-home-button>` placed in `.hud-header`, right after the drone switcher and before
  the `?` shortcuts button — literally adjacent to (directly below, when it's showing) the failsafe
  banner at the very top edge, and outside `.hud-controls`' Start/Stop/Replay row (a deliberate
  command, not a stream control). No new absolutely-positioned HUD island/offset math needed (the
  header row's own flex layout already has room), unlike `.hud-preflight`/`.hud-diagnostics`'s own
  "approximate, not computed" bottom-row offsets.
- **Command (`asset-panel.ts`/`.html`)**: `canBringHome` computed, fed from `marker()`'s own
  `firmware`/`sampleAgeSeconds` (no second `TelemetryStore` poller — the same reuse this panel's
  Mode/Armed/GPS/diagnostics facts already established) → `<vision-return-home-button>` appended to
  the existing `.row.panel-actions` (Watch live / Details / **Bring home**); `.panel-actions .btn { flex:1 }`
  applies to it for free through the `display: contents` host.
- **Deliberately not extracted as its own reducer**: the button's `busy` guard against
  double-fire/re-entrant clicks is a plain `signal(false)`/try-finally, not a pure state-machine
  file — a single one-shot request has nothing for a reducer to model beyond "in flight or not",
  unlike `player-recovery.ts`'s genuinely multi-transition reconnect saga.

### Tests

**994/994 → 1009/1009** (+15, all pure-logic; no new component-level specs for `ConfirmDialog`/
`ReturnHomeButton` — matches this codebase's own standing precedent for dumb HUD/panel pieces,
`FailsafeBanner`/`PreflightChecklist`/`DiagnosticsCard`/`AssetPanel` all have none either):
`core/telemetry/flight-state-logic.spec.ts` (+7 — `canCommandReturnHome`: ardupilot+fresh true,
wrong firmware false, no firmware false, stale false, no-sample false — the `isStale(undefined)`
divergence case specifically, both-absent false, case-sensitivity), `core/api/vision-api.spec.ts`
(+2 — `returnHome`'s empty-body POST + id-escaping), `core/map/map-logic.spec.ts` (+3 —
`FleetMarker.firmware` sourced/absent/offline-bucket-omitted, mirroring the existing
`flightMode`/`extra` describe blocks), `core/toast.service.spec.ts` (+2 — `warn()`'s kind/text,
the five-kind dismiss-ordering case extended to include `warning`), `shared/ui/return-home-button-logic.spec.ts`
(+2, new file — `returnHomeToastFor`'s two outcomes). `npm run test:ci` green; `npx tsc --noEmit`
clean on both `tsconfig.app.json`/`tsconfig.spec.json`.

### Build

`ng build --configuration production`: **340.00 kB raw / 96.01 kB transfer initial** — up only
+0.24 kB raw / +0.07 kB transfer from the prior recorded 339.76/95.94 (§G/§W's own baseline), the
same small, unavoidable tax every past cycle's new eager `VisionApi` method has paid (`returnHome`
+ `ToastService.warn`/the widened `ToastKind` + `.toast.warning`'s few CSS bytes in the
always-mounted `ToastHost` — all `providedIn: 'root'`/app-root-mounted, hence eager by construction).
**`ConfirmDialog`/`ReturnHomeButton`/`return-home-button-logic.ts` all stay lazy** — grep-verified:
`"no acknowledgement from aircraft"` appears in exactly one dist chunk, an unnamed shared chunk
reachable only from the `fly`/`command` lazy route chunks, absent from every initial chunk; `"Bring
home"` and `"return-home"` (the URL path fragment, from `VisionApi.returnHome`'s own eager method
body) are the only two strings from this wave found in an initial chunk, confirming the API-client
surface is the *only* eager addition, same as every past cycle's own verification method. Still
comfortably inside the 360 kB error budget.
